- **story-webnovel-planner**: 웹소설 연재 기획 스킬 — "어느 플랫폼에, 어떤 구조로 연재할 것인가"를 정하는 자리. 문피아·노벨피아·조아라·카카오페이지·네이버시리즈·리디 6개 플랫폼의 회차 분량 관행·결제 구조·태그 체계·독자층·프로모션 진입 조건·독점 여부를 비교해 타깃을 확정하고, 연재 구조 3분류와 레귤러/게스트 인원 예산을 설계한다. 회차 집필은 story-webnovel-writer에 위임한다. 다음과 같은 요청 시 반드시 이 스킬을 사용하세요: - "웹소설 기획", "웹소설 플랫폼 선택", "어느 플랫폼에 연재" - "문피아 연재", "노벨피아 연재", "조아라 연재" - "카카오페이지 소설", "네이버시리즈 소설", "리디 웹소설" - "웹소설 연재 구조", "웹소설 태그 전략", "독점/비독점" ([modu-ai/moai-cowork](https://github.com/modu-ai/moai-cowork/tree/HEAD/plugins/moai-story/skills/story-webnovel-planner) · ★302)
- **story-webnovel-writer**: 웹소설 회차 집필 스킬 — 한 회를 어떻게 쓰고 어디서 자를 것인가에 집중한다. 장르별 문법(현판·로판·무협·BL·미스터리)의 문단 길이·대사 비중·시점 관습·회귀/빙의/환생 클리셰 운용을 적용하고, 회차 절단(클리프행어) 강도를 등급으로 설계한다. 어느 플랫폼에 연재할지는 story-webnovel-planner에서 이미 확정된 것으로 본다. 다음과 같은 요청 시 반드시 이 스킬을 사용하세요: - "웹소설 회차 집필", "웹소설 한 회 써줘" - "웹소설 절단", "클리프행어 설계", "회차 끝 어떻게" - "현판 문법", "로판 문법", "무협 클리셰", "회귀/빙의/환생" - "연참 주기", "회차 분량 조율" ([modu-ai/moai-cowork](https://github.com/modu-ai/moai-cowork/tree/HEAD/plugins/moai-story/skills/story-webnovel-writer) · ★302)
- **story-webtoon-art**: 웹툰 작화 프롬프트 조립 스킬 — 화풍 앵커를 고정하고, 컷별 복붙 프롬프트를 조립하고, 첨부 이미지 서수를 1:1로 매핑하고, 멀티 이미지 세션 컨텍스트를 관리한다. 스타일 참조에서 피사체가 새어 그려지는 것을 내용물 가드로 막는다. 생성 실행·크레딧·모델 선택은 moai-media에 위임한다. 다음과 같은 요청 시 반드시 이 스킬을 사용하세요: - "웹툰 작화 프롬프트", "컷 프롬프트 조립" - "화풍 통일", "스타일 앵커", "참조 이미지 첨부 순서" - "캐릭터 일관성 프롬프트", "첨부 서수 매핑" - "멀티 컷 세션", "여러 컷 연속 생성" ([modu-ai/moai-cowork](https://github.com/modu-ai/moai-cowork/tree/HEAD/plugins/moai-story/skills/story-webtoon-art) · ★302)
- **story-webtoon-episode**: 웹툰 회차 스킬 — 회차 플롯을 짜고, 세로 스크롤 컷으로 분할하고, 컷마다 프레임 연출·인물 배치를 지시한다. 컷 수는 감정 비트가 정하고, 컷 높이와 여백 띠가 호흡을 만든다. 말풍선·식자는 story-webtoon-lettering으로 분리한다. 다음과 같은 요청 시 반드시 이 스킬을 사용하세요: - "웹툰 회차 대본", "컷 분할", "컷 나누기" - "웹툰 연출", "감정 컷 프레임", "인물이 컷 넘게" - "세로 스크롤 리듬", "여백 띠", "스크롤 호흡" - "회차 플롯", "훅 배치", "클리프행어" ([modu-ai/moai-cowork](https://github.com/modu-ai/moai-cowork/tree/HEAD/plugins/moai-story/skills/story-webtoon-episode) · ★302)
- **story-webtoon-lettering**: 세로 스크롤 웹툰의 식자 전담 스킬. 말풍선 종류를 고르고, 한 원고 = 한 서체를 스킬이 결정하고, 한국어 효과음(SFX) 스타일을 지정하고, 2패스 오버레이로 SFX를 안전하게 얹는 워크플로우를 제공한다. 말풍선·SFX 사전은 '사람이 읽는 설명'이 아니라 AI 이미지 프롬프트에 그대로 복붙하는 작동 토큰이다. AI 작화에서 한글 서체가 컷마다 흔들리는 1순위 품질 문제를 서체 통일 규칙으로 잡는다. 다음과 같은 요청 시 반드시 이 스킬을 사용하세요: - "말풍선 어떤 걸 써", "외침 풍선", "생각 풍선 프롬프트", "말풍선 꼬리" - "효과음 스타일", "쾅 SFX 어떻게", "한글 의성어 글자 스타일" - "웹툰 서체 통일", "글씨체가 컷마다 달라", "한 원고 한 서체" - "SFX 나중에 얹기", "2패스로 효과음", "효과음만 추가" - "식자 어떻게", "타이틀 서체", "간판 글씨 서체" - (체인 중간 호출) story-webtoon-episode·art·qc가 말풍선·서체·SFX 규격을 확인하려 할 때 ([modu-ai/moai-cowork](https://github.com/modu-ai/moai-cowork/tree/HEAD/plugins/moai-story/skills/story-webtoon-lettering) · ★302)
- **story-webtoon-planner**: 웹툰 기획 스킬 — "무엇을, 어떤 구조로 연재할 것인가"를 정하는 자리. 세계관·연재구조 3분류(에피소드 독립형/연속 서사형/혼합형)·시리즈 아크·관통 주제 1문장·훅 전략·레귤러 인원 상한을 설계한다. 플랫폼 데뷔 경로·회차 분량 같은 규격 사실은 story-webtoon-spec 허브를 참조하고 복제하지 않는다. 다음과 같은 요청 시 반드시 이 스킬을 사용하세요: - "웹툰 기획", "웹툰 세계관", "연재 구조 어떻게" - "시리즈 아크 짜줘", "웹툰 시즌 구성", "관통 주제" - "네이버웹툰 연재 기획", "카카오웹툰 기획" - "웹툰 캐릭터 몇 명", "레귤러 인원", "훅 전략" ([modu-ai/moai-cowork](https://github.com/modu-ai/moai-cowork/tree/HEAD/plugins/moai-story/skills/story-webtoon-planner) · ★302)
- **story-webtoon-qc**: 웹툰 산출 이미지 검수 스킬 — 그려진 그림이 지시대로 나왔는지 판정한다. 결함 7종을 확인 위치·수정 문구와 함께 점검하고, 세로 스크롤 규격 적합성과 일관성 앵커 위반을 판정한다. 썸네일로 놓치는 결함은 의심 컷만 crop 확대해 확인하고, 한 결함을 고치다 다른 것을 놓치는 터널비전을 막는다. 다음과 같은 요청 시 반드시 이 스킬을 사용하세요: - "이 컷 이상해", "생성 결과 검수", "웹툰 이미지 QC" - "캐릭터가 달라 보여", "무기 개수 이상해", "좌우 바뀐 것 같아" - "일관성 앵커 위반", "세로 규격 맞는지" - "쌍검 나왔어", "안경이 생겼어" ([modu-ai/moai-cowork](https://github.com/modu-ai/moai-cowork/tree/HEAD/plugins/moai-story/skills/story-webtoon-qc) · ★302)
- **story-webtoon-spec**: 한국 웹툰 플랫폼 규격의 단일 진실 원천(SSOT) 허브. 네이버웹툰 도전만화→베스트도전→정식연재 3단계 데뷔 경로, 세로 스크롤 원고 규격, 회차 분량 관행, 썸네일·표지 규격, 기다무·미리보기 수익화 훅, 실무 용어 사전을 한곳에 모은다. 단독으로도 답하고, 다른 story-* 스킬이 규격을 물어올 때 참조하는 상시 허브 역할을 한다. 다음과 같은 요청 시 반드시 이 스킬을 사용하세요: - "네이버웹툰 원고 규격 알려줘", "웹툰 캔버스 가로폭", "회차 세로 길이" - "도전만화 어떻게 올려", "베스트도전 승격 기준", "웹툰 데뷔 경로" - "카카오웹툰 투고", "네이버시리즈 표지 비율", "웹툰 썸네일 크기" - "기다무가 뭐야", "미리보기 유료 전환", "웹툰 회차 몇 컷" - "말칸 말고 정식 용어", "웹툰 용어 사전" - (체인 중간 호출) 다른 story-* 스킬이 원고 규격·데뷔 경로·용어를 확인하려 할 때 ([modu-ai/moai-cowork](https://github.com/modu-ai/moai-cowork/tree/HEAD/plugins/moai-story/skills/story-webtoon-spec) · ★302)
- **story-workflow**: Refine, analyze, improve, and groom user stories / work items for quality — readiness reports, template restructuring, adaptive gap-filling, and codebase-aware technical grooming. USER-ENTRY and HUMAN-ONLY — invoke only when the user explicitly runs /story-workflow &lt;command&gt;; never autonomously from conversation, never from a subagent (guard-enforced). - **strace-syscall-diagnosis**: 基于 strace/ltrace 的系统调用级故障诊断技能（双轨：调用轨迹 + 内核语义）。当用户提到 strace、ltrace、 系统调用、syscall、EACCES、ENOENT、ENOMEM、EAGAIN、进程卡住、futex 阻塞、epoll 超时、 read 卡顿、库函数追踪等关键词时，必须使用本技能。覆盖场景：syscall 错误码模式识别（EACCES/ENOENT/ EAGAIN/ENOMEM）、慢 syscall 定位（futex/epoll_wait/read 阻塞）、库函数调用链异常追踪、 进程 hang/blocked 在 syscall、文件描述符泄漏、资源泄漏模式等系统调用级故障。 即使用户只说"进程卡住了/系统调用很慢/函数调用异常"也要触发本技能。 支持内核源码级根因分析（有源码时必须走双轨并行并交叉验证）。 ([openeuler-mirror/witty-diagnosis-agent](https://github.com/openeuler-mirror/witty-diagnosis-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/strace-syscall-diagnosis) · ★5)

- **strat-operating-model**: 戦略の方向性が固まり、それを実行するためのオペレーティングモデルを設計したい場面で使用します。必要なケイパビリティの特定から、組織構造・業務プロセス・ガバナンス・人材・技術の各次元を設計し、現状とのギャップを明確にします。 ([Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public](https://github.com/Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public/tree/HEAD/skills/strat-operating-model))
- **strat-portfolio-review**: 複数の事業・製品・市場から成るポートフォリオを対象に、事業評価と資源配分の優先付けが必要な場面でお使いください。各ユニットの魅力度と競争優位を軸に Grow / Hold / Fix / Exit の方針を導き、選択と集中の意思決定を構造的に支援します。 ([Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public](https://github.com/Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public/tree/HEAD/skills/strat-portfolio-review))
- **strat-pricing**: 価格の設定・見直し、またはプライシングモデルの選定を行う場面で使用する。コスト積み上げ・バリューベース・競合基準の3方式を比較し、顧客の支払意欲とマージン・販売量のバランスを検討する。価格戦略の推奨案を成果物として生成する。 ([Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public](https://github.com/Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public/tree/HEAD/skills/strat-pricing))
- **strat-situation-assessment**: 市場・事業・オペレーション・組織の現状把握と診断を構造化したいときに使う。戦略的な意思決定の前に現状分析を行い、チーム全員が共有できる事実ベースのアセスメントを整理する。 ([Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public](https://github.com/Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public/tree/HEAD/skills/strat-situation-assessment))
- **strat-stakeholder-alignment**: 意思決定や変革推進において、ステークホルダーの影響力と関心を可視化し、関係者との合意形成をはかりたいとき、または関係者の巻き込みを設計したいときに使う。ステークホルダー調整計画を成果物として作成する。 ([Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public](https://github.com/Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public/tree/HEAD/skills/strat-stakeholder-alignment))
- **strat-strategic-options**: 戦略的な意思決定が未確定で、複数の選択肢を比較・評価する必要がある場面で使用します。明示的な評価基準に基づきオプションを構造化し、最善の選択肢を特定する意思決定分析を生成します。 ([Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public](https://github.com/Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public/tree/HEAD/skills/strat-strategic-options))
- **strat-transformation-roadmap**: 変革プログラムをフェーズに分けてロードマップ化し、ワークストリーム・マイルストーン・依存関係・早期成果を整理する場面で使用します。変革の方向性が定まり、実行計画を具体化する段階に入ったときに適用してください。 ([Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public](https://github.com/Acrosstudio-ai/Acrosstudio_pptx_skills-public/tree/HEAD/skills/strat-transformation-roadmap))
- **strat/blue-ocean**: Blue Ocean Strategy analysis (Kim & Mauborgne): Strategy Canvas mapping your offering vs competitors, ERRC Grid (Eliminate/Reduce/Raise/Create), Six Paths exploration, and a value innovation statement with copyability risk assessment. TRIGGER: "blue ocean", "oceano azul", "uncontested market space", "espaço de mercado por explorar", "ERRC", "strategy canvas", "/strat blue-ocean". SKIP: rating industry attractiveness and competitive forces -&gt; strat/five-forces (analyses the red ocean you are in); assessing defensibility of an existing position -&gt; strat/moat-analysis. ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/strategy/skills/blue-ocean) · ★16)
- **strat/bmc**: Business Model Canvas (Osterwalder): maps a business across the 9 blocks with a value proposition deep dive, delivering a completed BMC with VPC overlay and revenue model analysis. TRIGGER: "business model canvas", "BMC", "modelo de negócio", "desenha o canvas", "value proposition canvas", "revenue model", "/strat bmc". SKIP: testing whether an idea has real demand -&gt; saas/validate-idea (runs validation experiments, not canvas mapping); defining how to stand against competitors -&gt; strat/position. ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/strategy/skills/bmc) · ★16)
- **strat/extract-data**: Navigates a web page via browser integration and extracts structured data (tables, lists, prices, product listings) into CSV, markdown, or JSON, handling JS-rendered content and pagination, with WebFetch fallback for static HTML. TRIGGER: "extrai os dados desta página", "scrape this page", "extract the table", "apanha os preços do site", "export listings to CSV", "/strat extract-data &lt;url&gt;". SKIP: the ask is competitor insight rather than raw data -&gt; marketing/competitor-analysis or kb/competitive-intel (they interpret; this skill only extracts). ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/strategy/skills/extract-data) · ★16)
- **strat/five-forces**: Porter's Five Forces industry analysis: rates rivalry, threat of new entrants, supplier power, buyer power, and substitutes to produce an industry attractiveness verdict with strategic implications, optionally grounded in live competitor browsing. TRIGGER: "five forces", "cinco forças de Porter", "análise da indústria", "industry attractiveness", "atratividade do setor", "/strat five-forces". SKIP: deep dive on one competitor -&gt; marketing/competitor-analysis (company-level, not industry-level); escaping competition into new market space -&gt; strat/blue-ocean. ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/strategy/skills/five-forces) · ★16)
- **strat/growth-strategy**: Strategic growth-vector selection (Ansoff Matrix + Zook adjacencies + Greiner phases): decides WHERE to grow next with risk-adjusted feasibility and a 12-month roadmap with decision gates. TRIGGER: "estratégia de crescimento", "para onde crescer", "entrar num novo mercado", "diversificar", "growth strategy", "growth vector", "expand to a new market/segment/geo", "adjacency", "Ansoff", "moat", "defensibilidade", positioning-horizon questions about the business. SKIP: the vector is already chosen and the ask is the execution roadmap -&gt; saas/growth-plan (SaaS product, stage metrics) or community… ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/strategy/skills/growth-strategy) · ★16)
- **strat/moat-analysis**: Competitive moat analysis using Hamilton Helmer's 7 Powers: identifies which power applies to a company, scores how durable it is, and recommends concrete strengthening actions. TRIGGER: "moat", "análise de moat", "defensibilidade da vantagem", "vantagem competitiva duradoura", "7 powers", "how defensible are we", "/strat moat". SKIP: choosing WHERE to grow next -&gt; strat/growth-strategy (moat is one input to its vector selection); rating the whole industry's competitive forces -&gt; strat/five-forces. ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/strategy/skills/moat-analysis) · ★16)
- **strat/position**: Competitive positioning (Ries/Trout + April Dunford): maps competitive alternatives, unique capabilities, value, target segment, and market category into a positioning statement, perceptual map, and competitive differentiation, with browser-based competitor positioning scans. TRIGGER: "posicionamento", "positioning", "como nos posicionamos", "market category", "perceptual map", "diferenciação face à concorrência", "/strat position". SKIP: crafting the brand-voice positioning statement for messaging -&gt; brand/positioning-statement (brand expression; this defines the strategic position); moat du… ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/strategy/skills/position) · ★16)
- **strat/premortem**: Decision-quality ritual for irreversible bets, owned by the Governance squad: premortem before (assume failure, cluster causes, rank likelihood x impact, add mitigations or kill-criteria) and blameless postmortem after (timeline, 5 Whys to a system cause, lessons with owners). TRIGGER: "premortem", "pré-mortem", "e se esta aposta falhar", "one-way door decision", "blameless postmortem", "aprender do falhanço sem culpas", "/strat premortem". SKIP: maintaining a standing project risk log -&gt; pm/risk-register (continuous register, not a one-off ritual); production outage postmortems -&gt; dev/incide… ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/strategy/skills/premortem) · ★16)
- **strat/scenario-plan**: Scenario planning with PESTLE: builds 3-5 plausible futures (base, optimistic, pessimistic, black swan) and a strategy plus action plan for each, so the business is prepared for whichever unfolds. TRIGGER: "cenários", "scenario planning", "planeamento de cenários", "futuros possíveis", "e se o mercado mudar", "black swan", "/strat scenario". SKIP: numeric what-ifs on a financial model -&gt; finance/scenario-analysis (quantitative sensitivity on numbers; this is qualitative strategic futures); stress-testing a single bet -&gt; strat/premortem. ([andreagroferreira/arka-os](https://github.com/andreagroferreira/arka-os/tree/HEAD/departments/strategy/skills/scenario-plan) · ★16)
- **strata-contracts-and-infra**: Use when changing a GraphQL field / API operation / schema, or working with Lambdas and cloud resources, to find producers, consumers, and dead contract surface. Examples: "who implements this field?", "who consumes this operation?", "is this schema field dead?" ([markyarnold/stratagraph](https://github.com/markyarnold/stratagraph/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/strata/strata-contracts-and-infra) · ★1)
- **strata-exploring**: Use when the user asks how code works, wants the architecture, or to trace a flow across code/contract/infra. Examples: "how does the policy flow work?", "what calls this?", "trace this operation end to end" ([markyarnold/stratagraph](https://github.com/markyarnold/stratagraph/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/strata/strata-exploring) · ★1)
- **strata-guide**: Use first when you are unsure which StrataGraph tool to reach for, or need the tool/plane/band reference. Examples: "which tool do I use?", "what does StrataGraph index?", "how do I read confidence?" ([markyarnold/stratagraph](https://github.com/markyarnold/stratagraph/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/strata/strata-guide) · ★1)
- **strata-impact-analysis**: Use when the user wants to know what will break if they change something, or needs safety analysis before editing. Examples: "is it safe to change X?", "what depends on this?", "what will break?" ([markyarnold/stratagraph](https://github.com/markyarnold/stratagraph/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/strata/strata-impact-analysis) · ★1)
- **strata-scratchpad**: Use a persistent, cached Strata notebook as a scratchpad for throwaway Python — quick computations, data exploration, sanity checks, inspecting a value — instead of writing temp scripts or running `python -c`. Triggers whenever you are about to run ad-hoc Python to compute, inspect, or explore, and the `strata` CLI is available. ([bearing-research/strata](https://github.com/bearing-research/strata/tree/HEAD/plugins/strata-scratchpad/skills/strata-scratchpad) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **strata-status**: Get a quick overview of what Strata knows about the current project — session count, recent activity, key decisions, and knowledge health. ([kytheros/strata](https://github.com/kytheros/strata/tree/HEAD/templates/gemini-skills/strata-status) · ★1)
- **strata:archive**: Move merged branches' pr-context dirs into archive/. User-only — run when the user explicitly asks to "clean up old branches", "archive merged work", or "tidy the vault". Never deletes, only moves, but it mutates the vault and changes what recall returns, so it does not auto-invoke (matching forget / export-to-repo / promote-to-pr). Detects merged branches via `gh pr list --state merged` or `git branch --merged main` fallback. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/archive) · ★5)
- **strata:archive-planning**: Retire a `.planning/&lt;initiative&gt;/` subdir after its content has been migrated to the vault via `/strata:bootstrap`. Runs `git mv .planning/&lt;initiative&gt; .attic/&lt;initiative&gt;` and commits. Refuses to archive subdirs that aren't fully bootstrap-processed (to avoid losing knowledge). NEVER auto-invokes, always require explicit user request, since the operation rewrites the working tree. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/archive-planning) · ★5)
- **strata:audit-config**: Check the project's Claude Code config (CLAUDE.md, .claude/settings.json, skills, agents) for staleness. Read-only. Use when the user says "review our Claude config", "is our CLAUDE.md stale", "audit the setup", or after upgrading to a new model. Command-only — run it explicitly as /strata:audit-config. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/audit-config) · ★5)
- **strata:bootstrap**: One-time onboarding pass to seed the Strata vault from an existing codebase's docs. Scans CLAUDE.md, docs/, .planning/, and similar for candidates, then dispatches each one to a `strata:bootstrap-worker` subagent in parallel batches, the worker reads its file, classifies, writes the right kind of vault note, and returns one line. Idempotent, tracks processed files by SHA256. Use this once when first installing Strata on a repo with substantial existing documentation. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/bootstrap) · ★5)
- **strata:consolidate**: Promote durable notes out of aging per-branch pr-context dirs into the branch-agnostic lessons/ scope (rule-based, no LLM). Use when the user says "clean up old branch notes", "graduate these to lessons", "tidy pr-context", or after a wave of merged PRs. Dry-run by default; --apply to perform. Command-only — run it explicitly as /strata:consolidate. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/consolidate) · ★5)
- **strata:correct**: Edit, retire, or learn-from a vault note. Invoke autonomously when the user says "X note is wrong", "fix the part about Y", "update the status of &lt;note&gt;", "that's no longer true", "stop using &lt;note&gt;", "deprecate &lt;note&gt;" — OR corrects something Claude proposed ("no, X is actually Y", "that's wrong because Z", "we don't do it that way", "you got it backwards"). Finds the related note and applies the fix with conversation provenance (correction_source); if the corrected fact isn't in the vault yet, creates the right note instead. For ADR retirement prefer /strata:decide --supersedes; for deletio… ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/correct) · ★5)
- **strata:dashboard**: Show the current state of the Strata vault, scope counts, recent activity, drifted notes, stale ADRs, hot files, ADR↔commit linkage, suggested actions. Auto-invokable when the user says "show me the dashboard", "vault state", "what does strata see right now", "what's in the vault", or "give me a summary". Emits the same markdown that lives at `&lt;vault&gt;/&lt;repo&gt;/INDEX.md` (auto-regenerated on every write). No web server, no port, the dashboard IS a synced markdown file. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/dashboard) · ★5)
- **strata:decide**: Create a Markdown ADR in the vault's decisions/ scope. Invoke this autonomously the moment a non-trivial choice is made, a trade-off that locks something in, a constraint discovered the hard way, a choice between two reasonable options where future readers will wonder why. Don't wait for the user to ask. Pair with --supersedes when replacing an earlier decision. Skip for routine implementation choices fully explained by the code itself. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/decide) · ★5)
- **strata:doctor**: Health check for the Strata install. Verifies runtime packages, vault directory, repo namespace, search index, semantic search, MCP server, and git awareness — one glanceable checklist with a fix hint on every failure. Auto-invoke when the user says "is strata working", "did the install work", "strata seems broken", "is the vault set up", "health check", "why isn't recall finding anything", or "check my strata setup". Read-only — touches no notes, writes nothing. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/doctor) · ★5)
- **strata:domain**: Add or update a domain note in the vault's domain/ scope, vocabulary, invariants, conventions. Invoke autonomously when a domain term is defined or refined in conversation (e.g. "a Visit always belongs to exactly one Run", "events are past tense"). One concept per file, kebab-case filename, wikilink to related notes. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/domain) · ★5)
- **strata:eval**: Measure whether a retrieval/search change actually improved results, using a committed golden set (recall@k + MRR, fully local, no LLM judge). Use when the user says "did search get better or worse", "prove the ranking improved", "check for retrieval regressions", or "compare with and without rerank". Command-only — run it explicitly as /strata:eval. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/eval) · ★5)
- **strata:export-to-repo**: Promote a vault file (typically an ADR) into the host repo for git-blameable audit history. Use when the user asks to "make this ADR part of the codebase", "commit this decision to the repo", "promote to docs/adr/", "make this auditable", or for regulatory/compliance work that needs PR review. Lints with --strict before copy; never auto-commits, user runs git add / git commit themselves. Two-step with mandatory --dry-run for visibility. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/export-to-repo) · ★5)
- **strata:find**: Grep-style line-numbered text search across the vault — the literal-string fallback to the `recall` MCP tool. Prefer `recall` for ranked/semantic recall; use /strata:find after recall when the user explicitly wants literal grep hits with line numbers, or asks to "grep the vault" / "find the exact line that mentions X". ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/find) · ★5)
- **strata:forget**: Move a vault file to .trash/ + JSONL audit-log entry (who/when/why/SHA256). Recoverable, not destructive. Use when the user asks to "forget", "delete", "remove", "retract", "erase", "GDPR delete", "redact" a memory file, or when PHI/secrets accidentally landed in a note. Mandatory --reason argument captured in audit log. NEVER auto-invoke, always require explicit user request; the audit trail must reflect the user's intent. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/forget) · ★5)
- **strata:graphify**: Rebuild the code map so functions/classes show up in Obsidian's graph alongside decisions and domain notes. Use when the user says "rebuild the code graph", "the graph is stale", "update the code map after the refactor", or when /strata:review flags the graph as stale. Command-only — run it explicitly as /strata:graphify. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/graphify) · ★5)
- **strata:init**: One-time setup. Creates the vault namespace, then guides the user through the two optional add-ons that unlock the differentiated features. Installing Graphify (for code-graph awareness) and running bootstrap (to migrate existing planning docs). Interactive. Invoke when the user says "set up strata", "initialise the vault", "first install", or when a session starts in a repo where `&lt;vault&gt;/&lt;repo&gt;/` is empty. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/init) · ★5)
- **strata:lint**: Scan vault or staged files for credentials, PII, and (opt-in) PHI via pluggable JSON presets. Use when the user says "check for secrets", "scan for credentials", "is this safe to save", or mentions an API key/token/password/patient data. Runs automatically as a pre-step inside /strata:save, /strata:decide, and /strata:correct, so secret-scanning never depends on a probabilistic skill pick. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/lint) · ★5)
- **strata:observe**: Auto-capture a grounded, low-stakes OBSERVATION into the staging lane (status:auto, quarantined from recall until reviewed). The autonomous path is `/strata:save --observe` — that's the single autonomous-write decision point, so the model isn't choosing between save and observe on every write. This standalone command does the same capture for explicit use. NEVER for decisions / contested questions / domain definitions — those stay human-ratified. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/observe) · ★5)
- **strata:procedure**: Capture a procedural note, a recipe, workflow, checklist, or "how we do things here" template. Procedural memory is the third leg of the episodic/semantic/procedural triad; complements `domain/` (concepts) and `decisions/` (chosen options). Invoke autonomously when the user says "document this workflow", "save how we do X", "add a procedure for Y", "checklist for Z", or describes a step-by-step process worth keeping. Skip for one-off scripts, those belong in code or scratch. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/procedure) · ★5)
- **strata:promote-to-pr**: Post a session summary as a comment on the open PR for the current branch. Use when the user asks to "share this on the PR", "post a summary", "let reviewers know", "update the PR with what we did", or at the end of substantial work that reviewers should see. Requires `gh` CLI authenticated. Always two-step with --dry-run first. PR comments are visible to the whole team and shouldn't be sent on a single prompt's say-so. NEVER auto-invoke; always confirm with user before posting. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/promote-to-pr) · ★5)
- **strata:propose**: Capture an OPEN question or contested position that hasn't settled, tracking it open → contested → converging → settled. Invoke autonomously when the user says "we don't know yet whether X", "open question is Y", "we're split on Z", "I'm proposing X but not sure", "let's track this debate". Skip once a choice is locked in — for a decided option use /strata:decide; for a stable definition use /strata:domain. When it resolves, promote with --settled-as or --refuted-as. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/propose) · ★5)
- **strata:resume**: Re-print the Strata primer for the current branch (recent decisions, domain notes, PR-context, code-graph status). Invoke autonomously whenever the user asks "where did I leave off", "what's the current state", "pick up where we left off", "what's in flight on this branch", or when context has been compacted and you need fresh primer data. Cheap to run, preferred over re-reading raw files. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/resume) · ★5)
- **strata:review**: Vault health report, stale-proposed ADRs, stale durable notes (decayed + rarely recalled), orphan domain notes, missing frontmatter, unresolved wikilinks, stale PR-context dirs, stale Graphify code-graph. Read-only. Invoke autonomously when the user asks about vault hygiene, after a period of inactivity, or when starting a planning/cleanup session. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/review) · ★5)
- **strata:save**: Capture a session note. Two modes, direct (write immediately) and draft (build a draft from git session state, present for review, save on accept). Invoke autonomously at the end of substantive work (decisions made, files touched, context risks being lost). Pass `--draft` (or invoke when the user says "draft a save", "nudge me with a save", "what would you save") for the interactive review flow. Default is direct write. Skip if purely exploratory or &lt;5 turns. ([gideondk/strata](https://github.com/gideondk/strata/tree/HEAD/skills/save) · ★5)
- **strataudit**: Use when the user needs evidence-backed alignment analysis, traceability gaps, coverage, or a source-grounded evidence pack across strategy, architecture, design, or implementation documents in a real corpus. ([fall-out-bug/sdp](https://github.com/fall-out-bug/sdp/tree/HEAD/prompts/skills/strataudit) · ★19)
- **strategic**: strategic skills category for SAP Superpowers. Contains 5 sub-skills. ([vigneshbarani24/sap-superpowers](https://github.com/vigneshbarani24/sap-superpowers/tree/HEAD/skills/strategic) · ★9)
- **strategic-account-review**: Analyze and produce a strategic account review with structured process, quality checks, and system integration ([komunite/kalfa](https://github.com/komunite/kalfa/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sales/strategic-account-review) · ★245 · 3 copies)
- **strategic-action-agenda-design**: Create a high-stakes strategy by identifying the "crux" of a problem and designing a set of coherent actions to overcome it. Use this when a team is overwhelmed by a "laundry list" of 10+ priorities, when a product lacks a clear competitive edge, or when high-level goals (e.g., "grow 20%") are being mistaken for an actual plan.
